1. **Identify paths from M to B:**
   - Path 1: M → E → F → H → B
   - Path 2: M → E → F → K → H → B
   - Path 3: M → E → N → C → I → P → J → D → L → E → F → X → K → G → H → B
   - Path 4: M → E → L → D → J → P → I → C → N → B
   - Path 5: M → E → L → D → J → P → I → C → O → Y → N → B

2. **Critical nodes to cut all paths:**
   - From the paths identified, the nodes E and H are critical. Removing either E or H alone will not suffice because there are alternative paths. However, if we consider the Configuration: M → E → F → H → B, removing H alone will clearly disconnect this single linear path. For the alternative paths through N, Y, C, and P, while these paths exist they all go through H or E. Therefore, if we remove both nodes, we will fully disconnect all paths.

Thus, the minimum number of nodes to be removed to disconnect M and B is 2.
